([edheltzel/Do-Skills](https://github.com/edheltzel/Do-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/project/frontend/do-build-design-system) · ★2)
- **do-capital-gains-tax**: Dominican Republic Capital Gains Tax — accountant-verified rules from the DGII validation matrix (Miguel Lantigua, CPA Lic. 17839). ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/do-capital-gains-tax) · ★498)
- **do-chapter**: Run the whole AOFD (Vallis) pipeline for one chapter autonomously - preflight, analyze ∥ scout, curate (exhaustive coverage, tiered depth, K-recaps, 5–14 explainers with 3-D decisions, figures, movie groups), crossref to Kundu, design ∥ implement (fluidpy.geo/diag + aofd glue) ∥ data (fluidpy.data, disk guard), verify, review ∥ viz/viz3d ∥ figures ∥ notebook, knowledge ∥ library release ∥ publish, push - with parallel subagents where safe, gates, fix loops, progress tracking, a commit per phase and a C:/G: disk report per phase.
